\"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n    \n    // Initialize bodies (deterministically, identically on every rank)\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n    const Decomp dec(n, nprocs);\n    const int lo = dec.displs[rank];\n    const int cnt = dec.counts[rank];\n    std::vector<int> counts3(nprocs), displs3(nprocs);\n    for (int r = 0; r < nprocs; ++r) {\n        counts3[r] = 3 * dec.counts[r];\n        displs3[r] = 3 * dec.displs[r];\n    }\n\n    // Global positions (replicated), local velocities; interleaved xyz\n    std::vector<double> pos(3 * (size_t)n);\n    std::vector<double> vel(3 * (size_t)cnt);\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        pos[3 * i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n    }\n    for (int k = 0; k < cnt; ++k) {\n        vel[3 * k] = bodies[lo + k].vel.x;\n        vel[3 * k + 1] = bodies[lo + k].vel.y;\n        vel[3 * k + 2] = bodies[lo + k].vel.z;\n    }\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForcesLocal(pos.data(), vel.data(), n, lo, cnt);\n        integrateLocal(pos.data(), vel.data(), lo, cnt);\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, pos.data(), counts3.data(), displs3.data(),\n                       MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    // Collect velocities on root\n    std::vector<double> allVel(root ? 3 * (size_t)n : 0);\n    MPI_Gatherv(vel.data(), 3 * cnt, MPI_DOUBLE, allVel.data(), counts3.data(), displs3.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,\n                MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    if (root) printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Write back final state (positions on all ranks, velocities on root)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        bodies[i].pos = Vec3(pos[3 * i], pos[3 * i + 1], pos[3 * i + 2]);\n        if (root) bodies[i].vel = Vec3(allVel[3 * i], allVel[3 * i + 1], allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n    }\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && root) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n    \n    int ret = 0;\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        int ok = 0;\n        if (root) {\n            printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\\\n\");\n            ok = validateSimulation(bodies) ? 1 : 0;\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        \n        if (ok) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies, rank, nprocs);\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\\\n\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            if (root) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\\\n\");\n            ret = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return ret;\n}\n'''\nopen('nbody.cpp','w').write(src[:start]+main)\nEOF\nrm -rf build && cmake -B build -S . -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release >/dev/null && make -C build 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"\nfor p in 1 4 16 64; do mpirun --oversubscribe -np $p ./build/nbody -n 4000 -s 10 -r -v | grep -E \"time|Hash|energy\"; done"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3886,"cache_read_input_tokens":18890,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3886},"output_tokens":9,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"aeab2910-3325-4aff-9a32-15455ecca63d","uuid":"92f0b92b-9afc-45f0-85d0-7699ea850555","timestamp":"2026-10-03T17:48:49.662Z","request_id":"req_011CffiyrC4DrNE2J3txec8J"}

1 : 0;\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&ok, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        \n        if (ok) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies, rank, nprocs);\n            if (root) {\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\\\n\");\n            }\n        } else {\n            if (root) printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\\\n\");\n            ret = 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return ret;\n}\n'''\nopen('nbody.cpp','w').write(src[:start]+main)\nEOF\nrm -rf build && cmake -B build -S . -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release >/dev/null && make -C build 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\"\nfor p in 1 4 16 64; do mpirun --oversubscribe -np $p ./build/nbody -n 4000 -s 10 -r -v | grep -E \"time|Hash|energy\"; done","uuid":"2dfc4a69-08da-4fb2-9151-244d862c2a83","session_id":"aeab2910-3325-4aff-9a32-15455ecca63d"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CffjXZa4kdr6pwLD1xSyi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The nbody benchmark now runs on MPI in every build, and its positions and velocities come out bit-identical no matter how many processes it runs on. I only changed `nbody/nbody.cpp` and `nbody/CMakeLists.txt`, and the executable is still `nbody`.\n\n**How the work is split**\n- Each process takes a contiguous block of bodies (block sizes differ by at most one), so the work is evenly balanced.\n- Every process keeps a copy of all positions but only the velocities of its own bodies. A body's velocity only depends on positions, so one step is: compute forces for my bodies, update their positions, then one in-place `MPI_Allgatherv` that shares all the new positions. That's the only communication per step.\n- At the end, velocities are gathered to rank 0. Only rank 0 prints. The timer starts after a barrier and stops once results are collected, so it includes communication.\n- With `-v`, the potential-energy calculation is also split across processes (rows assigned round-robin to even out the triangular workload) and combined with `MPI_Reduce`.\n- The force loop processes 8 bodies at a time using the compiler's built-in vector types (no new library). Every body, including leftovers at the end of a block, goes through the same compiled code, which is why results don't depend on the process count.\n\n**One build-flag change affects results**\n- I added `-ffp-contract=off`. Without it, the compiler fuses multiplies and adds differently depending on how the code gets vectorized, so the original binary's own output depends on compiler choices. Without the flag I couldn't get identical results across process counts.\n- So the output hash differs from the original binary as originally built. It matches the original code built with `-ffp-contract=off` exactly; I checked this with n = 5, 1001, 4000 and 32768, on 1 to 128 processes.\n- I also added `-fno-math-errno`, which lets `sqrt` vectorize and doesn't change any results.\n- CMake now finds MPI and links `MPI::MPI_CXX`.\n\n**One output that varies slightly:** the \"Final energy\" line printed with `-v` adds up its partial sums in a different order depending on the process count. It can differ around the 6th decimal place (e.g. `…397264` vs `…397273`).\n\n**Performance (AMD EPYC 7763)**\n\n| Bodies × steps | Original | 1 process | 8 | 32 | 64 | 128 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 32768 × 5 | 40.6 s | 10.3 s | 1.30 s | 0.35 s | 0.19 s | 0.12 s |\n\nThat's about 4× faster on a single process from vectorization, and about 85× from 1 to 128 processes. These runs were all on one machine, not across a multi-node cluster.\n\nI also checked fewer bodies than processes, zero bodies, `-h`, and an unknown option. All behave correctly, apart from a cosmetic quirk: with an unknown option, every process exits with code 1, so `mpirun` adds its own \"job aborted\" message after the usage text."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":274,"cache_read_input_tokens":40967,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":274},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"aeab2910-3325-4aff-9a32-15455ecca63d","uuid":"6bf967f2-fa10-46a2-a2c0-a03768bbaad8","timestamp":"2026-10-03T17:55:52.832Z","request_id":"req_011CffjXYpRttAr555ca4W3j"}
